## 2026-10-29 03:07 CET — P1-DATA-CUSTOM-COMPOSITION-HARDENING root cause fixed; release QA pending

[PR #76 Quality 40803883982](https://github.com/saeedsai/ProMathStudio/actions/runs/40803883982) passed all prior structural and browser checks but exposed a **real numerical correctness defect** at `SUM(TWICE(1e308),2)`: the custom evaluator converted numeric arguments back into source strings, while the notation parser tokenizes `1e+308` as separate numeric/symbol/operator tokens rather than a scientific-notation literal. This could produce a finite but **incorrect** result instead of an overflow error. Fixed the evaluator bridge to bind numeric arguments directly to the custom function parameter environment, avoiding reparsing and preserving the existing depth/finite guards. Commits: core `12bf607c093a12c2c3117799cc76066c54fd33e2`, Chromium scientific-notation oracle `310c7dead2064659a595834d4e0dc2187a14c7cf`, structural guard `d78fc53ae4f8ae863fe6c4031dad24ebe3fe7ba3`, changelog `c7634e5f2cb6cd602f17f94102a6e09e76e55710`. **Exact-head CI and promotion are still pending; do not merge until green.** NEXT FIVE acceptance gates remain those below; historical inventory and recurring task remain active.

## 2026-10-28 15:00 CET — P1-DATA-TOOLBAR: TSV delimiter correctness and prior CI repair

**Feature ledger: IMPLEMENTED ON DEV; QA/RELEASE PENDING.** Fixed an actual Data Tables TSV round-trip defect: the shared graph/data CSV/TSV parser sniffs the delimiter from the header, but TSV export previously left commas/semicolons unquoted. **2026-10-28 11:09 CET — CI failure and root-cause repair.** [Quality 40669617199](https://github.com/saeedsai/ProMathStudio/actions/runs/40669617199) failed the newly mandatory `tests/smoke_sheet_median.py` assertion after inserting `=MEDIAN(A1:A5)` into a 3-row table; all preceding structural and browser steps passed. Root cause: the existing `value()` API returns numeric zero for out-of-data coordinates; range aggregation treated unpopulated rows as numeric zeros, distorting MEDIAN, AVERAGE and COUNT (SUM was unaffected). Fixed the range loop to skip unpopulated coordinates while retaining real numeric zero and existing bounds/area errors. Strengthened browser assertions for MEDIAN, COUNT and AVERAGE on A1:A5 and unit source guard. No CI bypass or weakened assertion. Exact repair SHA/CI, PR and Pages must be verified before release.

## 2026-10-28 06:55 CET — P1-DATA-JSON-SCHEMA-DRIFT (bounded lossless JSON import)

**DEV IMPLEMENTATION PENDING EXACT-SHA CI / PR / PAGES.** Fixed a silent JSON data-loss path shared by Data Tables and Graphs: object records with new keys in later rows previously lost those values because only the first record's keys were projected. # ProMathStudio — Hourly Development Plan & Execution Ledger

**Living plan** · initialized 2026-10-26 · authoritative operating rules: [AGENTS.md](../AGENTS.md).  
**Frozen scope baseline:** [26 Oct 2026 feature/gap audit](FEATURE_INVENTORY_AND_MATLAB_GAP_ANALYSIS_2026-10-26.md) — 158 current feature classifications (63 complete / 95 partial) and 166 missing/expansion opportunities. The audit is not a mathematical feature-count guarantee.

## Objective and workflow

Run **once every hour indefinitely**. Each run picks a narrowly bounded item, adds implementation + regression tests on **dev**, verifies exact SHA in CI, promotes via PR to **main** only after passing checks, validates deployment, documents progress, and refreshes the next five. Carry over unfinished items without downgrading their tests. The GitHub Pages site is published from `main`; the existing manual Pages workflow is optional. Do not confuse a green PR with verified production. The agent cannot guarantee uninterrupted execution in outages, platform limits or when safety/permissions prevent an action.

**Statuses:** TODO, IN PROGRESS, DEV IMPLEMENTED, CI GREEN, RELEASED, BLOCKED, PARTIAL.  
**Meaning of RELEASED:** exact code shipped to main, all required CI checks green, production deployment verified. Source change alone is not release.

New focused Playwright regression `tests/smoke_data_join_index.py` covers INNER/LEFT/RIGHT/FULL exact outputs, duplicate and empty keys, invalid keys, 10,000-result transactional rejection, 2,500-row unique joins, responsive widths and browser errors. Registered in mandatory Quality, synchronized modular/standalone source, updated Data Tables guide and changelog. Both branches unprotected. No unrelated branch cleanup needed.

## Higher-level release sequence

1. **Foundation and completeness — P0:** math typing/domains, symbolic systems, matrix operations, precision, limits, IVP numerical ODEs, composable Python, import/project schema resilience, XLSX, spreadsheet formula integrity and rigorous unit/browser/reference tests.
2. **Everyday numerical/scientific software — P1:** constrained optimization and fitting, vector calculus, interpolation, quadrature, statistics, parametric/polar/implicit/contour/vector plots, FFT and ODE conditions.
3. **Advanced scientific computing — P2:** eigen/sparse LA, DAE/BVP/stiff methods, multivariate optimization, signal/control, FEM/mesh workflows, scientific data formats and analysis tools.
4. **Broad ecosystem capabilities — P3:** parallel/GPU, model-based simulation, machine learning, image, engineering/industry modules. Break massive MATLAB/toolbox functionality into independently tested, explicitly scoped deliverables.
